{"forms":[{"form":"sheaves","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"sheafs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A quantity of the stalks and ears of wheat, rye, or other grain, bound together; a bundle of grain or straw."],"synonyms":[{"word":"reap"},{"word":"wheatsheaf"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"a sheaf of paper","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any collection of things bound together."],"synonyms":[{"word":"bundle"}]},{"glosses":["A bundle of arrows sufficient to fill a quiver, or the allowance of each archer."],"tags":["collective"]},{"glosses":["A quantity of arrows, usually twenty-four."],"tags":["collective"]},{"glosses":["A sheave."],"topics":["engineering","mechanical-engineering","mechanics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"glosses":["An abstract construct in topology that associates data to the open sets of a topological space (i.e. a presheaf) in such a way so as to make the local and global data compatible, generalizing the situation of functions, fiber bundles, manifold structure, etc. on a topological space. Formally, a presheaf ℱ whose sections are, in a technical sense, uniquely determined by their restrictions onto smaller sets: that is, given an open cover U_i of U:","If two sections over U agree under restriction to every U_i, then the sections are the same."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An abstract construct in topology that associates data to the open sets of a topological space (i.e. a presheaf) in such a way so as to make the local and global data compatible, generalizing the situation of functions, fiber bundles, manifold structure, etc. on a topological space.
